Let's implement fast full convolution function:
def conv_full(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); size=1<<( (n+m-2).bit_length() )
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size); B=np.fft.rfft(b,size); return np.fft.irfft(A*B,size)[:n+m-1]
For real. Might be slower for n=6121 than direct? FFT 16384 length, two rffts and multiplication. Probably comparable or faster if many.

Let's detail implement mirror descent.

Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np, time
from math import log, exp, sqrt, ceil

def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def make_fft_convolver(n, need_corr=False):
  Nc=next_pow2(2*n-1)
  Ncr=next_pow2(3*n-2)
  def conv_square(x):
    F=np.fft.rfft(x,Nc)
    y=np.fft.irfft(F*F,Nc)[:2*n-1]
    return y
  def corr_weights(p,w):
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,Ncr)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,Ncr)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fw*np.conj(Fp),Ncr).real
    return c[:n]
  return conv_square, corr_weights
```
Need check correlation: irfft(Fw*conj(Fp)) gives circular correlation of w and p with length N. For linear, c[m] = ∑_k w[k+m] p[k] for m=0..len(w)-1? I think yes with zero padding. Need maybe conjugate order: Standard ifft(fft(a)*conj(fft(b))) computes circular cross-correlation c_k =∑_n a_{n+k} \overline{b_n}. Let's test mentally with small using DFT: F_a[m]=∑a_n e^{-i 2πmn/N}, F_b=∑b_n e^{-...}. Product F_a conj(F_b)=∑_n∑_j a_n b_j e^{-i2πm n/N} e^{i2πm j/N}. ifft: 1/N∑_m ... e^{i2πm k/N}=∑_n,j a_n b_j δ_{k+j-n mod N}? Wait exponents: e^{-i2πm n/N} * e^{i2πm j/N} * e^{i2πm k/N} = e^{i2πm(j+k-n)}. Sum over m gives δ_{j+k-n mod N}. So result at k =∑_n,j a_n b_j δ_{n≡j+k} =∑_j a_{j+k} b_j. Yes c_k=∑_j a_{j+k} b_j (with wrapoff). Good. So corr = ifft(rfft(w)*conj(rfft(p))).real. Take first n. Great.

Convolution square: irfft(F*F) gives linear? With zero padding N>=2n-1, circular = linear. But rfft of real returns complex. Use `np.fft.irfft(F*F, Nc)[:2*n-1].reshape(-1)`. Might have tiny negative; clip c to 0 for weights? Conv of nonnegative should nonnegative but numerical tiny negative. Use np.maximum(c,0).

Scoring: score = 2*n*max(c)/1 (sum p=1). If p sum not 1 due to normalize, use evaluate or compute (sum)^2. We can keep sum1; for safety compute `2*n*max_c/(p.sum()**2)`.

Central initial:
```
def central(n):
  # recurrence a0=1; a_i = (1 - sum_{j=1}^{i-1} a_j a_{i-j})/2? Wait for c_i=1 with p0=1, c_i=2a0a_i + r_i =1 => a_i=(1-r_i)/2. r_i=∑_{j=1}^{i-1} a_j a_{i-j}. But this r_i can be computed from previous central coefficients? We can simply compute coefficients of (1-x)^-1/2: a_i = binom(2i,i)/4^i = a_{i-1}*(2i-1)/(2i). stable.
  a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
  for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
  return a/a.sum()
```
This is exact.

def smooth_weights(c, M, beta):
    # c nonnegative, sum1? c sum =1 for sum1.

```
If M small, beta=10 etc. Use `x = c/M`. For x<=0 -> -inf. logw = beta*log(x). For central x max=1, many x ~1; tail x can be 0. Good.
```
    x = c / M
    logw = np.full_like(c, -np.inf)
    pos = x > 1e-300
    logw[pos] = beta*np.log(x[pos])
    logw -= max(logw)
    w = exp(logw); w /= w.sum()
```
If beta large, only max. But if many equal max, uniform. Good.

Gradient:
```
g = 2 * corr_weights(p, w)  # length n
# remove p-weighted mean? For mirror, offset doesn't matter due to normalization, but subtract to control scale.
gbar = np.dot(p,g)
d = g - gbar
norm = np.linalg.norm(d) or max(abs(d))
if norm < eps: break
z = d/norm
```
Choose step alpha. We can use a schedule: start alpha=0.05. The log change = -alpha*z. Since z can have large max maybe >10 if d has outliers. We can clip z to [-zc,zc], e.g. zc=3. # If no improvement after few, maybe take a small noisy step or restart
```

Let's implement a generic mirrored gradient step:
```
def mirrored_step(p, g, alpha, clip=3.0):
    gbar = np.dot(p,g)
    d = g - gbar
    scale = np.max(np.abs(d))
    if scale ==0: return p, 0.0
    z = d / scale
    # We want if g high decrease; if g low increase. step = -alpha * z.
    # But if d positive large, p decreases. If d negative, p increases.
    # Normalize scale perhaps RMS: rms = sqrt(dot(p,d*d))
    # Use clip.
    logf = -alpha * np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
    pn = p * np.exp(logf)
    s=pn.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: fail
    return pn/s, alpha
```
Maybe choose alpha based on desired average relative change: if z clipped [-1,1] after RMS scaling, exp changes e^{±α}. Set α=0.1.

We can add to gradient: -g + (σ/p_i?)? Mirror step can include entropy by p update? Maybe not needed; if p_i underflow, set to tiny floor and renormalize.

    def conv_norm(p):
       F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
       # due to roundoff, nonnegative
       return np.maximum(c,0)
```

Mirror step:
```
    def step(p, g, alpha, clip=2.0):
       gbar = np.dot(p,g)
       d = g - gbar
       # scale by RMS with p weighting maybe
       rms = np.sqrt(np.dot(p, d*d))
       if rms <= 1e-300: return None
       z = d / rms
       # To avoid extreme, clip
       logf = -alpha * np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
       pn = p * np.exp(logf)
       s=pn.sum()
       if not finite or s<=0: return None
       return pn/s
```
Use RMS scale: If z clipped [-clip, clip] after z=d/rms, but some z > clip; clipping. RMS of p-weighted clipped about 1. The log changes at most α*clip ~0.2 if α=0.1. Good. Could set α=0.1, clip=3.

But if g has a large outlier causing d/rms huge, clipping compresses. Good.

Implementation specifics:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    import numpy as np
    import time
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    def now(): return time.monotonic()
    # choose n
    n = 6121
    parent = None
    try:
       arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if arr.ndim !=1: raise ValueError
       arr = np.clip(arr.reshape(-1),0,1000)
       if arr.size>0 and np.isfinite(arr).all() and arr.sum()>=0.01:
           parent = arr / arr.sum()
           if 100 <= parent.size <= 20000:
               n = parent.size
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    def central_seq(n):
       a=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64); a[0]=1.0
       for i in range(1,n):
           a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
       s=a.sum(); return a/s
```
If n too large for loop memory? okay.
Maybe vectorize central recurrence with cumulative product? Loop n=6121 fine.

Initial candidates:
```
    cands=[]
    cands.append(central_seq(n))
    if parent is not None and parent.size==n: cands.append(parent)
    # symmetric variants? central reversed (same as reverse) maybe p=(p+p_rev)/2 not feasible but candidate.
    # Add noise around central:
    for sig in [0.05,0.1]:
       p=central_seq(n)
       p=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sig,n)); p/=p.sum(); cands.append(p)
```
But creating many candidates before FFT. Could just central.

Set N:
```
    N = 1 << (int(3*n - 2) - 1).bit_length()  # need if m=1? n>1.
```
Python: `(3*n-2).bit_length()` gives bits for m; if m=8 (1000), (m-1).bit_length()=3 ->8. Use `1 << (m-1).bit_length()`.
If n=1, m=1, (0).bit_length()=0 ->1. But n large.

`conv` function:
```
    def convp(p):
        F=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)
        c[:2*n-1] = np.maximum(c[:2*n-1], 0.0)
        return c[:2*n-1]
```
Returning slice view? c[:2*n-1] is view of larger c? Actually `c = np.fft.irfft(..., N); c = c[:2*n-1].copy()?` If copy, memory. We can return view? It will keep full array alive if slice references? Yes slice view references full c; not issue. But we may later modify? We compute r = c - mu, doesn't modify. To save memory, we can do `c = np.fft.irfft(..., N); c = c[:2*nn-2]; c[c<0]=0` modifies full? okay. It keeps full N. For next call, overwritten. Fine.
But `np.maximum(c[:L],0)` returns new small array; do in-place:
```
        c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)
        c[:L] = np.maximum(c[:L], 0.0)
        return c[:L]
```
This returns view; `F*F` temporary complex N/2? okay.

Correlate:
```
    def corr(weight, p, Fp=None):
        if Fp is None: Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
        Fw=np.fft.rfft(weight,N)
        g=np.fft.irfft(Fw*np.conj(Fp), N)
        g = g[:n]
        return g
```
Need ensure weight length L clipped to N; weight length L<=N. Good.

In iterations, compute Fp once for conv and corr:
```
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,N); c[:L]=max; c=c[:L]
    ...
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(weight,N)
    g=np.fft.irfft(Fw*np.conj(Fp),N)[:n]
```
No separate Fp.

But after p update, Fp changes.

Mirror step with g and p. Need maybe exclude variables with p zero from gradient? g computed. If p_i extremely small, log update okay. We might clip z.

```
    def mirror(p,g,alpha,clip=2.0):
        gbar = np.dot(p,g)
        d = g - gbar
        rms = np.sqrt(np.dot(p, d*d))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-300: return None
        z = d / rms
        # if z has nonfinite, set 0
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        logf = -alpha * np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
        pn = p * np.exp(logf)
        # optional floor
        # pn = np.where(pn>0, pn, 0)
        s = pn.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
        pn /= s
        return pn
```
Potential issue: If p has zeros, gbar dot ignores; d for zero variables maybe nonzero, pz becomes zero*exp=0; fine. But support not revive. Random polish can.

Objective functions:
```
    L=2*n-1; invL=1.0/L
    def variance_from_c(c):
        mu = c.sum()*invL  # theoretical 1/L
        r = c - mu
        return float(np.dot(r,r)), r, mu
```
For gradient, r length L. Use same r.

Max soft:
```
    def soft_info(c, frac):
       M=float(c.max())
       if M<=0: return None
       tau=frac*M
       # logw = (c-M)/tau
       logw=(c-M)/tau  # <=0
       # To avoid underflow, exp(logw) okay if logw < -745 ->0. Use np.exp.
       w=np.exp(logw)
       s=w.sum()
       if not finite or s<=0: return None
       psi = M + tau*np.log(s)
       return w, psi, M
```
For central M~1e-4, tau=frac*M; c-M differences up to M, logw min -1/frac. If frac=0.05, min -20, fine. If frac=0.01, min -100, still. Good.

Main variance loop:
```
    best_p = initial_best.copy()
    best_score = score_from_c(convp(best_p))
    alpha=0.05
    stall=0
    while now() < var_until and stall<50:
       Fp=...
       c=...
       V,r,mu=variance(c)
       sc=2*n*c.max()
       if sc<best_score-1e-15: best=...
       # g
       Fw=np.fft.rfft(r,N)
       g=np.fft.irfft(Fw*np.conj(Fp),N)[:n]
       # Maybe if gradient norm small break
       gbar=np.dot(p,g)
       dnorm=np.sqrt(np.dot(p,(g-gbar)**2))
       if dnorm < 1e-20: break
       a=alpha
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(4):
          pn=mirror(p,g,a,clip=1.5)
          if pn is None: a*=0.5; continue
          c2=convp(pn)
          V2=np.dot((c2 - c2.sum()*invL)**2)
          sc2=2*n*c2.max()
          if V2 < V - 1e-13:
             p=pn; accepted=True
             if sc2 < best_score-1e-15: best_score=sc2; best_p=pn.copy()
             # increase alpha if good
             alpha = min(max(a*1.25, alpha), 0.3)
             stall=0
             break
          a *=0.5
       if not accepted:
          stall +=1
          alpha = max(a*0.5, 1e-5)
          # if many stall, random nudge
          if stall == 30:
             if best_score < inf:
                small = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)); small/=small.sum(); p=small.copy(); alpha=0.05; stall=0; continue? But this abandons current maybe. Could use.
```
Need avoid using `sc2` before c2 if sum pn not exactly1? It is normalized. Good.

`V` magnitude. Use tolerance relative: `V - 1e-12 * max(1.0, V)` maybe. V ~1e-5; 1e-12 absolute too small? We want acceptance of tiny improvements; use `1e-14`. Due to FFT noise ~1e-16, okay. Use `V2 < V - 1e-14` maybe. If V cannot improve by that, break.

But V may have local plateau where gradient nonzero but small. Random noise helps.

After variance phase, load `p = best_p.copy()` for max phase? If current p has lower V but worse score, maybe best score better. Max phase should start from best score. Use `p = best_p.copy()`.

Use functions:
```
    def compute_c(x):
       F=np.fft.rfft(x,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
       c[:L] = np.maximum(c[:L], 0.0)
       return c[:L]
    def compute_Fp(x): return np.fft.rfft(x,N)
    def conv_from_Fp(Fp): ...

Random polish:
```
    polish_until = deadline - 5
    sigma=0.1
    base = best_p
    fail=0
    while now() < polish_until:
       # If sigma too small, reset
       sigma = max(1e-4, min(0.5, sigma))
       # Try several perturb sizes
       p_try = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, n))
       # optionally symmetrize sometimes
       if (polish_iter % 10 == 0): p_try = 0.5*(p_try + p_try[::-1])
       p_try = np.clip(p_try,0,None); s=p_try.sum(); if s<1e-15: continue
       p_try/=s
       c=compute_c(p_try); sc=2*n*c.max()
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=sc; base=p_try.copy(); fail=0; sigma=min(sigma*1.05,0.5)
       else:
           fail+=1; sigma*=0.9
           if sigma<1e-4: sigma=0.1; base=best_p.copy() # restart
       # Maybe if no improvement for long, retry from central? 
```
This can be expensive; each try one FFT conv (2 transforms). 200s could do many. Good.
But random global noise may not improve if mirror optimum; still okay.

import numpy as np
import time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    try:
        rnd = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    except Exception:
        rnd = np.random.default_rng()
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1500.0
    deadline = start + max(5.0, budget - 5.0)

    # choose n and parent
    n=6121; parent=None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size>0 and arr.sum()>0.01:
            if 100 <= arr.size <= 20000:
                n = int(arr.size)
                parent = arr / arr.sum()
            else:
                # if available huge, maybe use min? Don't copy? We can truncate by taking first? But not needed.
                pass
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    def central_seq(m):
       a=np.empty(m,float); a[0]=1.
       for i in range(1,m): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
       s=a.sum(); return a/s
```

    def make_c(x):
        F = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)
        c[:L] = np.maximum(c[:L], 0.0)
        return c[:L], F
```

For max soft phase, line search objective `ψ`. Implementation:
```
p = best_p.copy()
for frac in (0.25,0.1,0.05,0.025,0.0125):
  if now() > deadline - 100: break
  alpha=0.03; stall=0
  for it in range(3000):
     if now() > deadline - 100: break
     Fp,c=make_c(p)
     M=float(c.max())
     if M<=0: break
     tau=frac*M
     z=(c-M)/tau
     # z<=0
     w=np.exp(z)
     ws=w.sum()
     if ws<=0 or not finite: break
     psi = M + tau*np.log(ws)
     sc=2*n*M; update best
     Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
     g=np.fft.irfft(Fw*np.conj(Fp),N)[:n]
     # line search with psi: compute for pn
     accepted=False; a=alpha
     for _ in range(4):
        pn=mirror(p,g,a,clip=1.5)
        if pn is None: a*=0.5; continue
        c2=make_c(pn)[0]
        M2=c2.max(); tau2=frac*M2; z2=(c2-M2)/tau2; w2=np.exp(z2); ws2=w2.sum(); psi2=M2+tau2*np.log(ws2); sc2=2*n*M2
        # accept if psi2 < psi + maybe and sc2 finite; prefer actual
        if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12 or psi2 < psi - 1e-13 or (sc2 <= best_score+1e-12 and psi2 < psi*0.999):
           p=pn; accepted=True; if sc2<best_score:...; alpha=min(max(a*1.2, alpha),0.2); stall=0; break
        a*=0.5
     if not accepted: stall+=1; alpha=max(a*0.5,1e-5); if stall>40: break
```
This may accept steps that don't improve ψ due to actual? Fine. But computing w2 each trial cost O(n), negligible.

def cg_solve(p, b, lam, iters=20):
    # Hx = p * G(p*x) + lam x
    # Precompute g and Fg from p
    Fp = rfft(p,N); G = irfft(Fp*conj(Fp),N)[:n]
    Fg = rfft(G,N); g0=G[0]
    def Hv(x):
       v = p*x
       Fv = rfft(v,N)
       conv = irfft(Fv*Fg,N)[:n]
       corr = irfft(Fv*np.conj(Fg),N)[:n]
       y = G_apply = conv + corr - v*g0
       return p*y + lam*x
    # diag estimate = Hv(e_i)? approximate p_i * (G(p e_i))_i + lam = p_i * (p_i*g0? plus contributions) maybe p_i^2 g0? G(p e_i) at i includes g0 p_i? G(v) for v=p e_i: y_i = v_i*g0 + ... = p_i g0; p_i*y_i=p_i²g0. diag_i=p_i²g0+lam.
    diag = p*p*g0 + lam
    r = -b - diag? Actually b. We can use scaled system to improve. Let B = diag maybe. Solve H u = -b. Use PCG: r = -b - H0.
```
But `lam` absolute. Let scale = np.mean(p*p*g0) (~1e-8). lam = 1e-3*scale? If too small, H singular. Use lam = 1e-6 + 1e-4*np.mean(p*p*g0).

CG iterates with preconditioner M=diag.
```
x = np.zeros(n)
r = -b - Hv(x)  #=-b
z = r/diag
pvec=z; rz=dot(r,z)
for k in range(iters):
   Ap = Hv(pvec)
   alpha = rz / dot(pvec,Ap)
   x += alpha*pvec
   r_new = r - alpha*Ap
   new_rz = dot(r_new, r_new/diag)
   if new_rz < 1e-12: break
   beta = new_rz/rz
   pvec = r_new/diag + beta*pvec
   r=r_new; rz=new_rz
```

def run_gn(p, max_time_frac, max_iters=2000, cg_iters=25, lam_rel=1e-5, alpha_start=0.35, stall=0):
   while time and stall<40:
      Fp,c=make_c_FP(p)
      V,r=variance(c)
      sc=...
      update best
      # maybe if score already very low? continue
      b = p * corr(r,Fp) # corr function using Fr and Fp
      u = solve_cg(p,b,cg_iters,lam_rel,Fp?) # might recompute g
      if u is None: break
      scale = np.linalg.norm(u)
      if scale<1e-12: break
      u = u/scale
      u = np.clip(u, -2.5, 2.5) # directional exaggeration? If clip after scale, max 2.5.
      accepted=False; al=alpha
      for _ in range(5):
          if al<1e-4: break
          pn=p*np.exp(al*u); pn/=pn.sum()
          c2=make_c(pn)[0]; V2=variance(c2)[0]; sc2=2n*c2.max()
          if V2 < V - 1e-13 or (sc2 < best_score - 1e-13):
              p=pn; accepted=True; update best; alpha=min(al*1.2, 0.8); stall=0; break
          al*=0.5
      if not accepted:
          stall+=1; alpha=al*0.5; lam_rel*=2? maybe
          if alpha<1e-5: break
   return p,best
```

def gn_direction(p, r, Fp):
   # compute b and solve, returns u_dir
   Fr=rfft(r,N)
   corr=irfft(Fr*conj(Fp),N)[:n]
   b=p*corr
   # compute g: irfft(Fp*conj(Fp),N)[:n]
   g=irfft(Fp*conj(Fp), N)[:n]; g=max(g,0)
   Fg=rfft(g,N); g0=g[0]
   mean_diag=np.mean(p*p*g0); lam=max(1e-100, 1e-5*mean_diag) # maybe 1e-5 too strong; try 1e-6

   def Hu(x): v=p*x; Fv=rfft(v,N); conv=irfft(Fv*Fg,N)[:n]; corrj=irfft(Fv*conj(Fg),N)[:n]; y=conv+corrj-v*g0; return p*y + lam*x
   # PCG
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=1500.0
    deadline = start + max(10.0, budget - 6.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n=6121; parent=None
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
       arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       if arr.size>0 and arr.sum()>0.01:
          if 64 <= arr.size <= 20000:
             n=int(arr.size); parent=arr/arr.sum()
    except: parent=None
    if not (64 <= n <= 20000): n=6121
    L=2*n-1; invL=1.0/L
    m=3*n-2
    N=1 << max(1, (m-1).bit_length())

    def central(m): ...

    base=central(n)
    def norm_clip(x):
       x=np.asarray(x,float)
       x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
       x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
       s=x.sum()
       if s<=1e-300: return None
       return x/s
    best_p=base.copy(); best_score=np.inf
    def c_of(x):
       F=np.fft.rfft(x,N); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N); c[:L]=np.maximum(c[:L],0); return c[:L]
    def fast_score(x):
       c=c_of(x); return float(2*n*np.max(c)), c
    def consider(x):
       nonlocal best_p,best_score
       p=norm_clip(x)
       if p is None: return
       sc,c=fast_score(p)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score-1e-15:
           best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy()
    consider(base)
    if parent is not None: consider(parent)
    # variants if budget enough
    if budget > 60:
       idx=np.arange(n,float)/(max(n-1,1))
       for gamma in np.linspace(-0.5,1.0,13):
           p=base*(1+gamma*(idx-0.5))
           consider(p)
       for h in (-0.3,-0.1,0.1,0.3,0.6):
           consider(base*np.exp(h*idx))
       for amp in (0.2,0.5,1.0):
           for phi in (0.0, np.pi, 0.5*np.pi, 1.5*np.pi):
               consider(base*np.exp(amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*idx+phi)))
```
The variants using central base normalized; if gamma negative and (1+gamma*(idx-0.5)) maybe positive for |gamma|<2. Good. If base*exp may have sum, consider normalizes.

Then objectives:
```
    def variance_parts(c):
       mu = c.sum()*invL
       r = c - mu
       return float(np.dot(r,r)), r
```
GN direction function:
```
    def gn_dir(p, r, Fp, cg_iters=30, lam_rel=1e-4):
       Fr=np.fft.rfft(r,N)
       corr=np.fft.irfft(Fr*np.conj(Fp), N)[:n]
       b = p * corr
       if not np.any(b): return None
       g = np.fft.irfft(Fp*np.conj(Fp), N)[:n]
       g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
       if g.size < n or g[0] <= 0: return None
       Fg=np.fft.rfft(g,N); g0=float(g[0])
       mean_diag=float(np.mean(p*p*g0))
       lam = max(1e-18, lam_rel*mean_diag + 1e-16)
       diag = p*p*g0 + lam
       diag[diag<=0]=lam
       def Hu(u):
          v=p*u
          Fv=np.fft.rfft(v,N)
          conv=np.fft.irfft(Fv*Fg,N)[:n]
          ccorr=np.fft.irfft(Fv*np.conj(Fg),N)[:n]
          y=conv + ccorr - v*g0
          return p*y + lam*u
       x=np.zeros(n)
       rv=-b
       z=rv/diag
       pv=z.copy()
       rz=float(np.dot(rv,z))
       if not finite or rz<=0: return None
       for _ in range(cg_iters):
          Av=Hu(pv)
          pav=float(np.dot(pv,Av))
          if not finite or pav <= 1e-300: break
          alpha=rz/pav
          x += alpha*pv
          rv -= alpha*Av
          rr=float(np.dot(rv,rv))
          if rr < 1e-24: break
          zn=rv/diag
          rzn=float(np.dot(rv,zn))
          if rzn <= 1e-300: break
          beta=rzn/rz
          pv=zn + beta*pv
          rz=rzn
       scale=float(np.linalg.norm(x))
       if not finite or scale<1e-300: return None
       return x/scale
```
Potential memory: In `Hu`, `Fv` alloc, conv, ccorr. Fine.

    p=best_p.copy()

         accepted=False; a=alpha
         for _ in range(5):
            if a < 1e-4: break
            pn = p * np.exp(a*u)
            s=pn.sum()
            if s>0: pn/=s

    while time < deadline - 3:
       op = rng.integers(0,3)
       p_try=p_best.copy()

          p_try *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
       elif op==1:
          width=int(rng.integers(max(2, n//500), max(3, n//20)+1))
          s0=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width+1)))
          p_try[s0:s0+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma*1.5, width))
       else:
          i=int(rng.integers(n))
          p_try[i] *= float(rng.choice(np.array([0.5,0.7,0.8,0.9,1.1,1.2,1.5])))
       p_try = np.maximum(p_try,0); s=p_try.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       p_try/=s
       c=c_of(p_try); sc=2*n*c.max()
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=sc; p_best=p_try.copy(); fail=0; sigma=min(sigma*1.1,0.5)
       else:
          fail+=1; sigma*=0.92
          if sigma < 1e-4: sigma=0.08
          if fail > 200: fail=0; sigma=0.2
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1500.0
    deadline = start + max(10.0, budget - 6.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n = 6121
    parent = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size >= 200 and arr.size <= 12000 and arr.sum() > 0.01:
            n = int(arr.size)
            parent = arr / arr.sum()
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if n < 200 or n > 12000:
        n = 6121

    L = 2 * n - 1
    invL = 1.0 / L
    N = 1 << max(1, (3 * n - 3).bit_length())

    def central(m):
        a = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
        a[0] = 1.0
        for i in range(1, m):
            a[i] = a[i - 1] * float((2 * i - 1)) / float((2 * i))
        s = a.sum()
        return a / s

    def norm_clip(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        s = x.sum()
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return None
        return x / s

    def c_of(x):
        F = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
        c[:L] = np.maximum(c[:L], 0.0)
        return c[:L]

    best_p = central(n)
    best_score = float("inf")

    def consider(x):
        nonlocal best_p, best_score
        p = norm_clip(x)
        if p is None:
            return
        c = c_of(p)
        sc = float(2 * n * np.max(c))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    consider(best_p)
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        consider(parent)

    if budget > 60:
        idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
        for gamma in np.linspace(-0.5, 1.0, 13):
            consider(best_p * (1.0 + gamma * (idx - 0.5)))
        for h in (-0.3, -0.1, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6):
            consider(best_p * np.exp(h * idx))
        for amp in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
            for phi in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi):
                consider(best_p * np.exp(amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * idx + phi)))

    def variance_parts(c):
        mu = float(c.sum() * invL)
        r = c - mu
        return float(np.dot(r, r)), r

    def gn_direction(p, r, Fp, cg_iters=20, lam_rel=1.0e-3):
        Fr = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(Fr * np.conj(Fp), N)[:n]
        b = p * corr
        if not np.any(b):
            return None

        g = np.fft.irfft(Fp * np.conj(Fp), N)[:n]
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        if g.size != n or g[0] <= 1e-300:
            return None
        g0 = float(g[0])
        Fg = np.fft.rfft(g, N)

        mean_diag = float(np.mean(p * p * g0))
        lam = max(1e-18, lam_rel * mean_diag + 1e-16)
        diag = p * p * g0 + lam
        diag[diag <= 0.0] = lam

        def Hu(u):
            v = p * u
            Fv = np.fft.rfft(v, N)
            conv = np.fft.irfft(Fv * Fg, N)[:n]
            ccorr = np.fft.irfft(Fv * np.conj(Fg), N)[:n]
            y = conv + ccorr - v * g0
            return p * y + lam * u

        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        rv = -b
        z = rv / diag
        pv = z.copy()
        rz = float(np.dot(rv, z))
        if not np.isfinite(rz) or rz <= 1e-300:
            return None

        for _ in range(cg_iters):
            Av = Hu(pv)
            pav = float(np.dot(pv, Av))
            if not np.isfinite(pav) or pav <= 1e-300:
                break
            alpha = rz / pav
            x += alpha * pv
            rv -= alpha * Av
            rr = float(np.dot(rv, rv))
            if rr < 1e-24:
                break
            zn = rv / diag
            rzn = float(np.dot(rv, zn))
            if not np.isfinite(rzn) or rzn <= 1e-300:
                break
            beta = rzn / rz
            pv = zn + beta * pv
            rz = rzn

        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        scale = float(np.linalg.norm(x))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
            return None
        return x / scale

    if budget > 30:
        p = best_p.copy()
        alpha = 0.30
        lam_rel = 1.0e-3
        stall = 0
        gn_until = deadline - 240.0
        if budget < 400:
            gn_until = deadline - 40.0

        while time.monotonic() < gn_until and stall < 80:
            Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
            c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, N)
            c[:L] = np.maximum(c[:L], 0.0)
            c = c[:L]
            V, r = variance_parts(c)
            sc = float(2 * n * np.max(c))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()

            if not np.isfinite(sc) or (np.isfinite(best_score) and sc > 1.4 * best_score + 1e-8):
                p = best_p.copy()
                alpha = 0.2
                lam_rel = 1.0e-3
                continue

            if V <= 1e-20:
                break

            u = gn_direction(p, r, Fp, cg_iters=20, lam_rel=lam_rel)
            if u is None:
                stall += 2
                lam_rel = min(1.0, lam_rel * 2.0)
                if stall > 20:
                    break
                continue

            u = np.clip(u, -3.0, 3.0)
            accepted = False
            a = alpha
            tol = max(1e-15, 1e-9 * V)
            for _ in range(6):
                if a <= 1e-5:
                    break
                pn = p * np.exp(a * u)
                s = pn.sum()
                if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                    a *= 0.5
                    continue
                pn /= s
                c2 = c_of(pn)
                V2, _ = variance_parts(c2)
                sc2 = float(2 * n * np.max(c2))
                if (np.isfinite(V2) and V2 < V - tol) or (np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13):
                    p = pn
                    accepted = True
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                        best_score = sc2
                        best_p = pn.copy()
                    alpha = min(1.0, max(a * 1.15, 1e-4))
                    lam_rel = max(1e-5, lam_rel * 0.8)
                    stall = 0
                    break
                a *= 0.5

            if not accepted:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(a * 0.5, 1e-5)
                lam_rel = min(1.0, lam_rel * 1.5)
                if stall == 35:
                    p = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
                    p /= p.sum()
                    alpha = 0.15
                    lam_rel = 1.0e-3
                    stall = 10

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        p = best_p.copy()
        alpha = 0.07
        stall = 0
        mirror_until = deadline - 80.0

        while time.monotonic() < mirror_until and stall < 70:
            Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
            c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, N)
            c[:L] = np.maximum(c[:L], 0.0)
            c = c[:L]
            V, r = variance_parts(c)
            sc = float(2 * n * np.max(c))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()

            if not np.isfinite(sc) or (np.isfinite(best_score) and sc > 1.3 * best_score + 1e-8):
                p = best_p.copy()
                break

            Fr = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
            g = np.fft.irfft(Fr * np.conj(Fp), N)[:n]
            gbar = float(np.dot(p, g))
            d = g - gbar
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(p, d * d)))
            if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-300:
                break

            z = np.clip(d / rms, -2.0, 2.0)
            accepted = False
            a = alpha
            tol = max(1e-15, 1e-9 * V)
            for _ in range(5):
                if a <= 1e-5:
                    break
                pn = p * np.exp(-a * z)
                s = pn.sum()
                if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                    a *= 0.5
                    continue
                pn /= s
                c2 = c_of(pn)
                V2, _ = variance_parts(c2)
                sc2 = float(2 * n * np.max(c2))
                if (np.isfinite(V2) and V2 < V - tol) or (np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13):
                    p = pn
                    accepted = True
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                        best_score = sc2
                        best_p = pn.copy()
                    alpha = min(0.6, a * 1.15)
                    stall = 0
                    break
                a *= 0.5
            if not accepted:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(a * 0.5, 1e-5)
                if stall == 40:
                    p = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n))
                    p /= p.sum()
                    alpha = 0.1
                    stall = 10

    polish_until = deadline - 4.0
    sigma = 0.08
    fail = 0
    while time.monotonic() < polish_until:
        op = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
        p_try = best_p.copy()
        if op == 0:
            p_try *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
        elif op == 1:
            width = int(rng.integers(max(2, n // 500), max(3, n // 20) + 1))
            s0 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            p_try[s0:s0 + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.5 * sigma, width))
        else:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            f = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.5, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.1, 1.2, 1.5])))
            p_try[i] *= f

        p_try = np.where(np.isfinite(p_try), p_try, 0.0)
        p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 0.0)
        s = p_try.sum()
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        p_try /= s
        c = c_of(p_try)
        sc = float(2 * n * np.max(c))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p_try.copy()
            fail = 0
            sigma = min(0.6, sigma * 1.1)
        else:
            fail += 1
            sigma *= 0.92
            if sigma < 1e-4:
                sigma = 0.08
        if fail > 300:
            fail = 0
            sigma = 0.2

    final = [float(x) for x in best_p]
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(final))
    except Exception:
        val = float("inf")

    fallbacks = []
    try:
        b = central(n)
        fallbacks.append(b)
    except Exception:
        pass
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        try:
            fallbacks.append(parent)
        except Exception:
            pass

    for cand in fallbacks:
        pc = norm_clip(cand)
        if pc is None:
            continue
        lst = [float(x) for x in pc]
        try:
            cv = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            cv = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(cv) and cv < val:
            val = cv
            final = lst

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        final = [1.0] * n
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(final))
        except Exception:
            val = float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            final = [1.0]

    return final
```
